<script>
   /* CSS rel=preload polyfill. Depends on loadCSS function. [c]2016 @scottjehl, Filament Group, Inc. Licensed MIT  */
(function (w) {
   // rel=preload support test
   if (!w.loadCSS) {
      return;
   }
   var rp = loadCSS.relpreload = {};
   rp.support = function () {
      try {
         return w.document.createElement("link").relList.supports("preload");
      } catch (e) {
         return false;
      }
   };

   // loop preload links and fetch using loadCSS
   rp.poly = function () {
      var links = w.document.getElementsByTagName("link");
      for (var i = 0; i < links.length; i++) {
         var link = links[i];
         if (link.rel === "preload" && link.getAttribute("as") === "style") {
            w.loadCSS(link.href, link);
            link.rel = null;
         }
      }
   };

   // if link[rel=preload] is not supported, we must fetch the CSS manually using loadCSS
   if (!rp.support()) {
      rp.poly();
      var run = w.setInterval(rp.poly, 300);
      if (w.addEventListener) {
         w.addEventListener("load", function () {
            w.clearInterval(run);
         });
      }
      if (w.attachEvent) {
         w.attachEvent("onload", function () {
            w.clearInterval(run);
         })
      }
   }
}(this));</script>

<script>
   var searchTitles = {
      md: c24.getData('searchtitleMD'),
      sm: c24.getData('searchtitleSM'),
      xs: c24.getData('searchtitleXS'),
      noWatch: c24.getData('searchtitleNoWatch')
   };

   $(window).load(function () {
      var query = $('#query');

      query.c24Autocomplete({
         url: c24.getData('watchSuggestUrl'),
         resultContainer: '#jq-ac-results',
         noHitsSuggestText: c24.getTranslation("label.pressEnterToSearch"),
         useMaxAvailableWidth: true,
         clearSearchStorageText: c24.getTranslation('search.filters.clear-search-history'),
         overlaySelector: ".jq-header-search",
         userInput: query.val().trim(),
         sendEmptyMask: true,
         trackSearchSubmit: true,
         initialTabs: [
            {
               id: 'recentSearches',
               name: c24.getTranslation('search.filters.recent-searches'),
               endpointUrl: null,
               searchStorage: true,
               sourceData: function() {
                  return getSearchStorageAsXml();
               },
               order: c24.meta.isNewVisitor && !getSearchStorage().length ? 2 : 1,
               onSelect: function(tab) {
                  tce(tab.id, 'search-tab');
               }
            },
            {
               id: 'topSearches',
               name: c24.getTranslation('search.filters.top-searches'),
               endpointUrl: c24.getData('searchTopUrl'),
               order: c24.meta.isNewVisitor && !getSearchStorage().length ? 1 : 2,
               onSelect: function(tab) {
                  tce(tab.id, 'search-tab');
               }
            },
         ],
         tabs: [
            {
               id: 'watches',
               endpointUrl: c24.getData('watchSuggestUrl'),
               name: c24.getTranslation('watchtypes.U'),
               parameters: {
                  watchTypes: '',
                  accessoryTypes: '',
               },
               hideAutocomplete: false,
               selected: c24.getData('topSearchSelectedTab') == 'watches',
               onSelect: function (tab) {
                  tce(tab.id, 'search-tab');
               }
            },
            {
               id: 'parts',
               endpointUrl: c24.getData('accessorySuggestUrl'),
               name: c24.getTranslation('watchtypes.Z'),
               parameters: {
                  watchTypes: 'Z',
                  accessoryTypes: '',
               },
               hideAutocomplete: false,
               selected: c24.getData('topSearchSelectedTab') == 'parts',
               onSelect: function (tab) {
                  tce(tab.id, 'search-tab');
               }
            },
            {
               id: 'bracelets',
               endpointUrl: c24.getData('bandStrapSuggestUrl'),
               name: c24.getTranslation('accessorytypes.2000'),
               parameters: {
                  watchTypes: 'Z',
                  accessoryTypes: '2000',
               },
               hideAutocomplete: false,
               selected: c24.getData('topSearchSelectedTab') == 'bracelets',
               onSelect: function (tab) {
                  tce(tab.id, 'search-tab');
               }
            },
            {
               id: 'magazine',
               endpointUrl: c24.getData('suggestUrl'),
               name: c24.getTranslation('header.navigation.magazine'),
               hideAutocomplete: false,
               selected: c24.getData('topSearchSelectedTab') == 'magazine',
               onSelect: function (tab) {
                  tce(tab.id, 'search-tab');
               }
            },
         ],
      });

      $(window).resize(function () {
         setQueryPlaceholder();
      });
      setQueryPlaceholder();

      $('#language-switch-header').on('click', function () {
         c24.colorbox.createJsonp(c24.getData('localizationChangeUrl'), {size: "md"});
      });
   });

   $(window).load(function () {
      $('.i-delete', '#header-search').click(function () {
         var instance = $('#query').get(0).ac;

         if (instance != null) {
            $('#query').val('').focus().keyup();
         }
      });
   });

   function setQueryPlaceholder() {
      var searchTitle = searchTitles[c24.tools.responsiveSize()];

      if (searchTitle != null) {
         $('#query').attr('placeholder', searchTitle);
      }
   }

   function storeSearchStorageModel() {
      if('searchStorageModel' in c24.meta.data) {
         var searchStorage = [];
         var newEntry = c24.meta.data.searchStorageModel;

         try {
            if(localStorage.getItem('searchStorage') != null) {
               searchStorage = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('searchStorage'));

               searchStorage.forEach(function(entry, index) {
                  if(newEntry.label === entry.label && newEntry.explanation === entry.explanation) {
                     searchStorage.splice(index, 1);
                  }
               });
            }

            if(String(newEntry.label).trim().length) {
               searchStorage.unshift(newEntry);
            }

            searchStorage = searchStorage.slice(0, 5);

            localStorage.setItem('searchStorage', JSON.stringify(searchStorage));
         } catch (e) {
            // empty
         }
      }
   }

   function getSearchStorage() {
      try {
         return localStorage.getItem('searchStorage') ? JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('searchStorage')) : [];
      } catch (e) {
         // c24.errors.logWarning('Error in search', 'search-js.jsp.inc', 0, 0, e);
         return [];
      }
   }

   function getSearchStorageAsXml() {
      var xml = "<root>";

      getSearchStorage().forEach(function(entry) {
         if(entry.originalPath) {
            xml += "<option url='" + entry.originalPath + "' filter-text='" + entry.explanation + "'>" + entry.label + "</option>";
         } else {
            xml += "<option url='" + c24.getData('searchIndexUrl') + entry.searchParams + "&dosearch=true' filter-text='" + entry.explanation + "'>" + entry.label + "</option>";
         }
      });

      xml += "</root>";

      return xml;
   }

   function getSelectedTab() {
      var query = $('#query');
      return query.get(0).ac != undefined ? query.get(0).ac.getCacheId() : 'watches';
   }

   $(document).ready(function () {
      $('#query').click(function () {
         tce(getSelectedTab(), 'search-input');
      });

      $('#header-search button').click(function () {
         tce(getSelectedTab(), 'search-submit-typed');
      });

      storeSearchStorageModel();
   });
</script></div>

<script>
      $(document).ready(function () {
         function positionOutsideDirectionNav() {
            var slideHeight = $('#jq-trust-pilot-slider').find('li').eq(0).innerHeight();
            var arrowHeight = $('.jq-outside-direction-nav').find('.control-outside').eq(0).innerHeight();
            var offset = (slideHeight / 2) - (arrowHeight / 2);

            $('.jq-outside-direction-nav').css('top', offset);
         }

         c24.flexslider({
            containerId: 'jq-trust-pilot-slider',
            controlNav: true,
            directionNav: true,
            startCallback: function (slider) {
               positionOutsideDirectionNav();
               slider.resize();
            }
         });

         $(window).on('resize', function () {
            positionOutsideDirectionNav();
         });
      });
   </script>

<div class="m-y-6">
<div class="row row-compressed same-height keep-linebreaks">
<div class="col-sm-12 col-md-6 m-b-2">
<div class="rcard d-flex flex-column relative l-2">
<div class="rcard-body">
<h2 class="h4">
         Luxury Watches</h2>
<div>
<p>
               Luxury watches are a source of fascination for people around the globe. But what qualifies as a luxury watch? Some may say it's any watch made of expensive materials such as gold or platinum. Others could argue that it has to be a mechanical watch with a handmade movement. Then there are those who define luxury based on legendary brands such as Rolex, Patek Philippe, and Breitling.<br/><br/>Whatever it is you're looking for, you will find luxury watches from all the famous brands in every price range on Chrono24. Find and purchase your dream watch now.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-12 col-md-6 m-b-2">
<div class="rcard d-flex flex-column relative l-2">
<div class="rcard-body">
<h2 class="h4">
         Used Watches</h2>
<div>
<p>
               Not every watch has to come fresh from the factory. In fact, many people fall for the charm of watches with designs from the 60s or 70s. These vintage watches include coveted collector's items and hidden treasures waiting to be uncovered by watch fans. Here you will find both expensive rarities as well as legendary pieces whose predecessor models can be purchased at affordable prices.<br/><br/>Searching for the right vintage watch is part of the overall shopping experience and often turns novices into watch aficionados.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-12 col-md-6 m-b-2">
<div class="rcard d-flex flex-column relative l-2">
<div class="rcard-body">
<h2 class="h4">
         Sell Watches</h2>
<div>
<p>
            Every month, over 7 million watch enthusiasts search for their next watch on Chrono24 – perhaps yours is the watch they are looking for? Create a free listing in a few simple steps and find a buyer for your watch.<br/><br/>Once you've made a sale, the rest is easy: The buyer transfers the purchase price to a secure escrow account and then you ship the watch. Once it has arrived safely at its destination, you will receive payment into your bank account. We retain a small commission fee at the time of payout.<br/><br/>Looking to free up some wrist space? Sell your watch now on Chrono24.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-12 col-md-6 m-b-2">
<div class="rcard d-flex flex-column relative l-2">
<div class="rcard-body">
<h2 class="h4">
         Buying a Watch</h2>
<div>
<p>
               Found your dream watch on Chrono24? Don't hesitate to make this dream a reality. The safest way to do so is via Trusted Checkout. You transfer the purchase price into an escrow account, and then the seller sends you your watch. We only pay out the sum to the seller when you have the watch in your hands.<br/><br/>What's more: Purchases made via Trusted Checkout already include Chrono24's Buyer Protection and Authenticity Guarantee. If you pay the purchase price directly to the seller, you can still activate these services after the fact.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div></div>

<script type="text/javascript">
         $(document).ready(function () {
            var hasSeenContactBox = c24.data.getString('97') === '1' || false,
               contactBox = $('#jq-contact-box'),
               contactBoxToggler = $('#jq-contact-box-toggle'),
               closeContactBox = $('.jq-close-contact-box'),
               isWebtest = 'false' === 'true',
               isCheckoutMediation = false,
               hideContactBox = false;

            if(c24.hasData('hideContactBox')) {
               hideContactBox = c24.getData('hideContactBox');
               if(hideContactBox) {
                  $('#jq-sticky-contact-box').addClass('d-none');
               }
            }

            if(c24.hasData('isMediationContact')) {
               isCheckoutMediation = c24.getData('isMediationContact');

               if(isCheckoutMediation) {
                  $('.js-contact-link').on('click', function (e) {
                     e.preventDefault();
                     $('#complaintFormSubmit').click();
                  });
               }
            }

            if (c24.tools.userlikeIsOnPage()) {
               contactBoxToggler.remove();
               contactBox.remove();
               return;
            }
            contactBoxToggler.on('click.toggleContactBox', toggleContactBox);
            closeContactBox.on('click.toggleContactBox', toggleContactBox);

            var animationTimeout = null;

            function animateIn(element) {
               element.removeClass('invisible box-out').addClass('box-in');
            }

            function animateOut(element) {
               element.removeClass('box-in').addClass('box-out')

               clearTimeout(animationTimeout);

               animationTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
                  element.addClass('invisible');
               }, 1000);
            }

            function toggleContactBox() {
               if (!contactBox.hasClass('box-in')) {
                  tce('sticky-widget-module', 'sticky-widget-maximize');
                  animateOut(contactBoxToggler);
                  animateIn(contactBox);
               }
               else {
                  tce('sticky-widget-module', 'sticky-widget-minimize');
                  animateIn(contactBoxToggler);
                  animateOut(contactBox);
               }
            }

            if (!hasSeenContactBox && !c24.meta.isMobile && !isWebtest) {
               animateIn(contactBox);

               c24.layer.show(function () {
                  var firstVisitorAnimation = setTimeout(function() {
                     animateIn(contactBoxToggler);
                     animateOut(contactBox);
                  }, 10000);

                  closeContactBox.one('click.cancelFirstVisitorAnimation', function() {
                     clearTimeout(firstVisitorAnimation);
                  });

                  c24.data.setString('97', '1', 31536000);
               }, 1300);
            } else {
               contactBoxToggler.addClass('box-static-in');
            }
         });
      </script>

         $(document).ready(function () {
            $('.countries a').click(function () {
               var a = $(this);
               var language = a.data('l');
               var currency = a.data('c');
               this.href = this.href + ((this.href.indexOf('?') == -1) ? '?' : '&') + 'SETLANG=' + encodeURI(language) + '&SETCURR=' + encodeURI(currency);

               var country = language.substr(3, 2);
               if (country == 'GB') country = 'UK';
               tce('home.index', 'country-link-' + country);
            });
         });
